> AGENT

Skipped `desloppify` per your request and did a manual subjective review.

**Findings**
1. [P1] External-file drag/drop path is less robust than internal drag/drop: it awaits mutation without `try/catch` and exits before `onDropHandled`, so failures can bubble and the overlay-finalization path can be skipped.  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/hooks/useAppExternalDrop.ts:94`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/hooks/useAppExternalDrop.ts:97`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/hooks/useAppExternalDrop.ts:109`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/hooks/useAppExternalDrop.ts:126`

2. [P2] Abort-signal composition in Supabase fetch wrapper does not handle an already-aborted caller signal before `fetch`, so canceled requests can still run until timeout.  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/integrations/supabase/bootstrap/createSupabaseClient.ts:25`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/integrations/supabase/bootstrap/createSupabaseClient.ts:30`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/integrations/supabase/bootstrap/createSupabaseClient.ts:35`

3. [P2] Debug globals are wired into normal app runtime (not clearly dev-gated), which increases side effects and weakens production cleanliness.  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/App.tsx:24`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/mobileProjectDebug.ts:25`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/Layout.tsx:20`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/debugPolling.ts:124`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGallery/index.tsx:162`

4. [P3] Global safety posture is still transitional (`strict: false`, broad lint relaxations), so architecture intent is stronger than enforcement across the whole repo.  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/tsconfig.app.json:18`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/eslint.config.js:22`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/eslint.config.js:27`

5. [P3] Core UX flows remain very large orchestration units; this is explicitly acknowledged in-code, so maintainability is improving but not yet “beautiful.”  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.tsx:79`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.tsx:82`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotImagesEditor.tsx:1`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaLightbox/VideoLightbox.tsx:1`

**Holistic verdict**
Not holistically beautiful yet, but it is clearly on an engineering-upgrade path. The architecture direction is good (modularization, boundaries, strict islands), but runtime debug leakage, uneven robustness in critical paths, and still-large orchestrators keep it in “strongly improving” rather than “finished craftsmanship.”

**What I validated**
1. `npm run lint:strict-islands` passed.
2. `npm run check:no-smoke-tests` passed.
3. Targeted tests passed: `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/main.test.tsx`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGallery/index.test.tsx`, `/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.test.tsx`.

> AGENT

Implemented all five findings, without running the `desloppify` scanner.

1. External drop robustness fixed  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/hooks/useAppExternalDrop.ts:94`  
- External-file drops are now wrapped in `try/catch`.  
- `onDropHandled()` now always runs for accepted external drop targets (via `finally`), so overlay cleanup is consistent on success/failure.

2. Abort composition hardened in Supabase fetch wrapper  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/integrations/supabase/bootstrap/createSupabaseClient.ts:19`  
- Added explicit `composeAbortSignals(...)` helper.  
- Handles already-aborted caller signals correctly and cleans up listeners in `finally`.

3. Debug tooling is now explicitly dev-gated and centralized  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/bootstrap.tsx:30`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/App.tsx:1`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/app/Layout.tsx:1`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/debugPolling.ts:123`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/mobileProjectDebug.ts:24`  
- Removed unconditional debug side-effect imports from app/layout entry paths.  
- Dev-only debug imports are now initialized in bootstrap.  
- Both debug modules now explicitly gate global registration behind `import.meta.env.DEV`.

4. Enforcement tightened (incremental, non-breaking)  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/package.json:32`  
- Expanded `lint:strict-islands` scope to include Supabase bootstrap + debug modules + extracted gallery debug hook.  
- I tested a strict TS island approach, but it pulled transitive repo-wide strict debt; I did not leave a failing command behind.

5. Large-component decomposition started (real extraction, not just comments)  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGallery/hooks/useMediaGalleryDebugTools.ts:1`  
`/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/MediaGallery/index.tsx:137`  
- Moved debug-specific logic out of `MediaGallery` into a dedicated hook (`useMediaGalleryDebugTools`).  
- `MediaGallery` now focuses more on UI/data orchestration.

Validation run:
- `npm run lint:strict-islands` passed  
- `npm run check:no-smoke-tests` passed  
- `npx vitest run src/app/main.test.tsx src/shared/components/MediaGallery/index.test.tsx src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/index.test.tsx` passed (6/6)  
- `npm test` passed (279 files, 2679 tests)

> DEVELOPER

Continue working through all of these: 

Findings

[P1] External-file drag/drop path is less robust than internal drag/drop: it awaits mutation without try/catch and exits before onDropHandled, so failures can bubble and the overlay-finalization path can be skipped.
useAppExternalDrop.ts (line 94)
useAppExternalDrop.ts (line 97)
useAppExternalDrop.ts (line 109)
useAppExternalDrop.ts (line 126)

[P2] Abort-signal composition in Supabase fetch wrapper does not handle an already-aborted caller signal before fetch, so canceled requests can still run until timeout.
createSupabaseClient.ts (line 25)
createSupabaseClient.ts (line 30)
createSupabaseClient.ts (line 35)

[P2] Debug globals are wired into normal app runtime (not clearly dev-gated), which increases side effects and weakens production cleanliness.
App.tsx (line 24)
mobileProjectDebug.ts (line 25)
Layout.tsx (line 20)
debugPolling.ts (line 124)
index.tsx (line 162)

[P3] Global safety posture is still transitional (strict: false, broad lint relaxations), so architecture intent is stronger than enforcement across the whole repo.
tsconfig.app.json (line 18)
eslint.config.js (line 22)
eslint.config.js (line 27)

[P3] Core UX flows remain very large orchestration units; this is explicitly acknowledged in-code, so maintainability is improving but not yet “beautiful.”
index.tsx (line 79)
index.tsx (line 82)
ShotImagesEditor.tsx (line 1)
VideoLightbox.tsx (line 1)

Continue working through all of these: 

Findings

[P1] External-file drag/drop path is less robust than internal drag/drop: it awaits mutation without try/catch and exits before onDropHandled, so failures can bubble and the overlay-finalization path can be skipped.
useAppExternalDrop.ts (line 94)
useAppExternalDrop.ts (line 97)
useAppExternalDrop.ts (line 109)
useAppExternalDrop.ts (line 126)

[P2] Abort-signal composition in Supabase fetch wrapper does not handle an already-aborted caller signal before fetch, so canceled requests can still run until timeout.
createSupabaseClient.ts (line 25)
createSupabaseClient.ts (line 30)
createSupabaseClient.ts (line 35)

[P2] Debug globals are wired into normal app runtime (not clearly dev-gated), which increases side effects and weakens production cleanliness.
App.tsx (line 24)
mobileProjectDebug.ts (line 25)
Layout.tsx (line 20)
debugPolling.ts (line 124)
index.tsx (line 162)

[P3] Global safety posture is still transitional (strict: false, broad lint relaxations), so architecture intent is stronger than enforcement across the whole repo.
tsconfig.app.json (line 18)
eslint.config.js (line 22)
eslint.config.js (line 27)

[P3] Core UX flows remain very large orchestration units; this is explicitly acknowledged in-code, so maintainability is improving but not yet “beautiful.”
index.tsx (line 79)
index.tsx (line 82)
ShotImagesEditor.tsx (line 1)
